# מדע וטכנולוגיה יסודי - חומרי הוראה ולמידה: 76 topic pages and their linked resources

Source: https://pop.education.gov.il/tchumey_daat/mada-tehnologia/yesodi/noseem_nilmadim/

Saved: 2026-10-13. These are the Ministry "teaching and learning materials" topic pages. Pages marked **GRADE 6** are returned by the site filter for grade 6. Each entry lists the resources the page links to (Ministry files, plus external sites such as TAU MATAR, Weizmann, Eureka, YouTube). External links were NOT downloaded (not Ministry documents); Ministry files were downloaded where listed in INDEX.md.
